# Findings 0 — Sean's feedback, pass by pass, from the milestone 2 build on

Each section is one pass of Sean's feedback: what he saw, and what changed for it, with tidy passes between. The first, below, is his first pass at the milestone 2 build; the file grew from there, so its name stayed. A pass saved before a compaction says so in its heading, and says where it was built.

**Passes.** Launch and the MVP: the first to the sixteenth, with the hygiene pass and two tidies (2026-10-06 to 07). The keyboard, the phone, new categories: the seventeenth to the twenty-third, with three tidies (2026-10-07 to 08). Side panels, Units, and Display: the thirty-fifth to the forty-sixth, with three tidies (2026-10-15). The newest pass is at the bottom.

Date of the first pass: 2026-10-06. One person, the product owner, on the desktop build in the Theorem theme. Not a usability test; the observations were acted on the same day so the five-person test (usability-test-1.md) starts from a cleaner baseline.

## Observations and what changed

| Observation                                                             | Change                                                                                               | Where                                           |
| ----------------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------- |
| The unit chip sat left of the number; "mL 250" reads backwards          | Chip moved to the right of the number                                                                | `SmartInput.tsx`                                |
| After opening a category, the way back was at the bottom of a long list | Back control at the top of the panel; "Search all categories" lives there too                        | `SuggestionPanel.tsx` header                    |
| Escape should mean "back" at every step                                 | Escape ladder: expanded list → unit → panel → text                                                   | `SmartInput.tsx` `escape()`, UX-SPEC §9         |
| No undo or redo; undo should target the last interaction, not the text  | History with typing runs coalesced; Ctrl+Z / Ctrl+Y and two buttons; undoing a lock keeps the number | `state.ts` `reduceWithHistory`, `Converter.tsx` |
| Clicking the chip did nothing                                           | Chip body opens the unit list for its category; × removes                                            | `UnitChip.tsx`                                  |
| "category" subtext in rows was noise                                    | Removed; chevron and layout carry the meaning                                                        | `SuggestionPanel.tsx`                           |
| "Neon Noir" broke the sentence-case rule                                | Renamed "Neon noir"; copy rules written down                                                         | `themes.ts`, DESIGN-SYSTEM "Copy"               |

## Open questions for the five-person test

Set aside with the test (2026-10-08); the open questions now are in docs/UX-SPEC.md §15.

- Does Escape removing the unit surprise anyone who only meant to close the list?
- Do people find undo without the keyboard shortcut? The buttons are small and sit by the hint.
- Is a chip on the right discoverable as something to click?

## Second pass, same day

| Observation                                                                    | Change                                                                                                 |
| ------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| The list pushed content down                                                   | Floats over the page                                                                                   |
| Should Enter finalize like clicking out?                                       | Yes: Enter picks or locks, then closes the list; focus stays in the field                              |
| Chip hover matched the field background                                        | Hover tints the chip toward the surface                                                                |
| Only six to eight digits fit                                                   | Wider page, one-step smaller type past nine characters, symbol-only chip past six                      |
| Should both boxes copy?                                                        | Yes, whenever a box holds a number                                                                     |
| Too much text in the list: chevron, "Search all categories", "Now pick a unit" | Plain "← Back" only when a category is expanded; scoped searches widen on no match; the prompt is gone |
| Escape removed the unit when typing was the last thing done                    | Escape clears the most recently entered thing first                                                    |
| The theme dropdown looked unlike the inputs                                    | Trigger restyled to the shared control language; native popup kept                                     |

## Third pass, same day

| Observation                                                        | Change                                                                                                          |
| ------------------------------------------------------------------ | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| The list the chip opens had no way back to the categories          | Every unit list has "← Back" with the category name; Back and Escape both go up to the categories               |
| "gb" and "ml" chipped themselves while still being typed           | No lock on a keystroke; the match is highlighted and Enter, a pick, Tab, or leaving the field commits it        |
| Escape closed the list when Back was showing                       | Escape is Back whenever Back is visible; it closes only at the category level                                   |
| Nothing said which category you were in                            | A label above the boxes names the category being browsed or locked, and clears with the unit                    |
| The detached list and the square native theme popup broke the look | Field and list are one fluid shape again, drawn over the page; the theme menu is built the same way with radios |

## Fourth pass, same day (recorded before a context reset, built after it)

| Observation                                                                                               | Change                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Row hover and selected highlights, and the scrollbar, overlap the shell's two-pixel edge and look clipped | The edge is a real `border-2` (line at rest, accent when open or focused) that children cannot paint over; rows sit in a `p-1` gutter with `rounded-md` corners, and the thin scrollbar sits inside the same gutter. Same for the theme menu. Checked with the last row hovered in every theme |
| Escape clears the whole text; typing in bursts should be separate events, and undo should match           | Keystrokes less than 700 ms apart form one chunk; a pause or a space starts a new one. Undo, redo, and Escape all step through those chunks; Escape steps are themselves undoable (UX-SPEC §9)                                                                                                 |
| Unit rows read "symbol name"; wanted the name on the left and the abbreviation right-aligned              | Rows read `name … symbol`, symbol right-aligned in muted numeric type. The chip keeps "symbol name"                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| Category rows appear for alias and substring matches and make the list feel inconsistent                  | A category row needs the start of its name ("vol" → Volume) or a whole alias ("fluids"); no prefix or substring on aliases, no fuzzy categories (`classifyCategory`, UX-SPEC §4)                                                                                                               |
| An `E` in a value blends in with the digits                                                               | The exponent marker is semibold, drawn by a text layer over the field (UX-SPEC §7, DESIGN-SYSTEM "Fields")                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| Long numbers get cut off; two or three size steps are not enough                                          | Five size steps chosen by measuring the text against the field on every change and resize; the number stays on one line and scrolls only past the smallest size. Wrapping is a later idea                                                                                                      |
| (Found while checking) Hover was invisible in Daylight                                                    | `--surface-raised` in Daylight was the same white as `--surface`; now a faint warm gray                                                                                                                                                                                                        |

Noticed, not changed: the URL stores the parsed number (`v=123456789012345680` after typing eighteen digits), so a reload shows the double rather than the typed text. Fold into the URL and persistence work in milestone 3.

| Observation                                                                                | Change                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| Symbols in the list were not bold like the chip                                            | Symbols are semibold, right-aligned, in the chip's style                                                                                                                                                     |
| A result too large to compute showed "0"                                                   | The other box says "Too large to convert" or "Too small to convert" in place of a value; numbers beyond a double count as numbers (UX-SPEC §7)                                                               |

Above the boxes, with undo and redo at its right                                                                  |

Even 8 px insets, nested corners, 2 px edges |
| A way to clear everything                                                                  | An eraser button at the far right of the toolbar; undoable; puts the caret back in the first box                                                                                                             |

| Back should be reachable with the arrow keys                                               | Back is the list's first option, pinned while scrolling; Up from the first unit or Down from the last highlights it and Enter goes back                                                                      |
| "Neon noir" should be "Neo-noir"                                                           | Renamed, id `neo-noir` included                                                                                                                                                                              |

| Swap and the keyboard icon should look like the clear button                               | Both are ghost icon buttons: no border, the page behind them                                                                                                                                                 |
| A green edge lingered around the keyboard icon after its menu closed                       | The icon uses the button focus ring, and the open edge comes and goes with the panel, without a fade                                                                                                         |

## Hygiene pass, same day

Two read-only audits (docs against code, code hygiene) ran on 2026-10-06. Code fixes: visible focus on every button (Tailwind v4's `outline-none` was hiding the ring), header menus closing when focus leaves them, arrow keys previewing themes without closing the menu, reduced motion that actually zeroes the theme tokens, the URL keeping a German "1.000" as 1000, a 44 px chip remove target, motion and corner utilities in the bridge, one fluid-shell recipe (`src/lib/shell.ts`), one timing module (`src/lib/timing.ts`), shared e2e helpers (`e2e/support.ts`), a gallery of real states, dead code removed, and a test for each. Every doc was then brought to current behavior: UX-SPEC, usability-test-1, README, ARCHITECTURE, TESTING, DESIGN-SYSTEM, THEMING, PLAN, UNITS-CATALOG, LEARNINGS (superseded notes and new entries), ADRs 0002 and 0006 (outcome notes), and CLAUDE.md (rules 6 and 7, recipes).

## Sixth pass: three decisions, two small changes, ideas for later

The audit left three questions for the owner.

| Question                                                 | Decision                                                                                            | Change                                                                                                                                                           |
| -------------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Field edges are under 3:1 against the page (WCAG 1.4.11) | Try a slightly darker edge, and roll it back if it does not look right                              | A new `--edge` token for control edges at the smallest step past 3:1 in every theme; `--line` stays for dividers. In its own commit, so a rollback is one revert |
| Should Tab or clicking away commit an exact match?       | No. Only Enter or a pick commits, so tabbing and clicking around never changes what was typed       | Tab and blur commit nothing; back in the box, the best match is highlighted again so Enter takes it (UX-SPEC §5)                                                 |
| A lint guard for raw values?                             | Flag every deviation, match it to what exists or propose a token, and bring it to the owner in chat | A token guard in `pnpm lint` (DESIGN-SYSTEM, "Token guard")                                                                                                      |

| Observation                                                                                   | Change                                                                                                                                                                               |
| --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| Back from a category should land on that category                                             | Back and Escape highlight the category just left. Rows now take the highlight on pointer movement, not entry, so a row that appears under a still mouse cannot steal it (UX-SPEC §3) |
| "Toggle boxes" should read "Toggle inputs"                                                    | Renamed in the shortcuts map                                                                                                                                                         |
| (Found while checking) In the second box, typing "vol" and picking Volume listed Volume again | Entering a category drops the typed word in either box, not only in the source box                                                                                                   |

Logged for later in UX-SPEC §14, "Owner's ideas": a copy-link button in the header, the keypad as a calculator with a switch for which box it fills, a header animation of conversions on hover, and art for each theme beside the site name.

| Request or observation                                                                                               | Change                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Copy link, between the keyboard icon and the theme menu, confirming with the same check                              | Built (UX-SPEC §7). The converter draws it into a slot the header reserves and builds the address from its own state, so the link is current even before the address bar is                                                         |
| Is the space between the keyboard and link icons the same as between the link icon and the theme menu?               | It was not: 36 px between glyphs, 22 px from the link glyph to the menu's edge, because ghost icons sit 12 px inside their buttons. The menu now sits 22 px after the icons, 36 px both ways (DESIGN-SYSTEM, "Space by what shows") |

Core's parser reads arithmetic (§2); Enter and = turn it into its value (§5); its running value shows in the field (§7)    |
| The header animation: leisurely while hovered, faster the faster the pointer moves                                   | Logged with that detail (UX-SPEC §14), next with theme art                                                                                                                                                                          |
| Theme art: original images in each theme's colors                                                                    | Confirmed: SVG drawn for bigsmall in each theme's tokens (UX-SPEC §14)                                                                                                                                                              |
| (Found while checking) After copy link the header wrapped on a 375 px phone, and the hint was squeezed to five lines | Below `sm` the theme menu shows just the theme's name and narrows, so the header fits a 360 px phone; the hint takes the full width with the buttons on a line under it                                                             |

Budget: 85.7 KB of the 90 KB JavaScript budget is used after this pass. The header animation must stay small; theme art as SVG in the static HTML costs no JavaScript.

Same pass, after Sean saw the first calculator:

| Request or observation                                                                                                                                                     | Change                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| The calculator should have its own value area: a scratch pad where sums are tried, then sent to the 1st or 2nd box, replacing its number or filling it, or not used at all | The calculator keeps its own entry with a display and running value; "Use in 1st" and "Use in 2nd" send the value, one undoable step that keeps the box's unit. The box switch, the targeted-box edge, and the hidden phone keyboard are gone (UX-SPEC §8)                |
| Arithmetic typed into the boxes without the calculator should keep working                                                                                                 | Kept: Enter as equals and the running value in the field (UX-SPEC §5, §7)                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| The keyboard shortcuts map glitched to a green outline again when it closed after a hover                                                                                  | The hygiene pass's shared shell recipe had brought back the edge transition the earlier fix removed. Motion now lives only in the bordered variant, a browser test fails if the ghost edge can animate, and LEARNINGS records how merging recipes undid the fix           |
| Keep everything systemized: a variant, token, or component rather than a one-off                                                                                           | Checked every new piece: `Button` gained `on` (a ghost button switched on), the toolbar's rule became `ToolbarDivider`, the number sizes moved to `NUMBER_SIZES`, the operator keys use a `soft` variant, and the calculator is one `Calculator` component in the gallery |

## Eighth pass: the calculator, refined

| Request or observation                                                                               | Change                                                                                                                                                                            |
| ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Typing should go to the calculator while it is open; Enter pressed the calculator icon and closed it | Opening moves focus onto `=`; typed keys press calculator keys while focus is inside; clicking any key brings focus back (UX-SPEC §8)                                             |
| Enter should be = unless Tab moved to a send button, and Tab should cycle =, Use in 1st, Use in 2nd  | Tab and Shift+Tab cycle =, the two send buttons, and close; Enter presses the focused one; the send buttons stay in the cycle, dimmed, when there is nothing to send              |
| Escape should step back like the boxes: the latest burst, then the one before, then close when empty | Same 700 ms bursts as the boxes; = and C start over; Escape on an empty display closes                                                                                            |
| Clicking outside should not close it; the icon and a close button should                             | Clicking elsewhere leaves it open; a header with the title and a close button; the icon toggles it; closing keeps the entry                                                       |
| The display was far too large; it should be the size of the inputs                                   | The display is a field-sized box (same edge, height, and type sizes) with the running value inline (`12×3 = 36`)                                                                  |
| Backspace should remove a value                                                                      | Backspace presses ⌫                                                                                                                                                               |
| An E key and a `^` key, and `^` in the boxes too                                                     | `^` in core's parser for boxes and calculator (`2^10`, `−2^2` is −4); an E key for times ten to the power of; a power with no real value says "No real answer"                    |
| C, E, ⌫, and the decimal mark in a slightly different color                                          | A `tonal` Button variant from existing tokens (raised surface, muted ink); no new token needed                                                                                    |
| Undo and redo should work in the calculator, from a send back into the sum                           | The calculator's entry moved into the converter's state and its one history: undo after a send takes back the send, then the =, then the typing                                   |
| (Owner, then withdrawn) Show the full number whenever it fits, and E only when it does not           | Kept as is: exponent form from 1e15 or below 1e-6. Noted as a possible later refinement, with the caution that whole numbers past 10 significant digits would show invented zeros |

## Ninth pass: the wordmark at play and theme art

| Request                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     | Change                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| A header that plays on hover with a ton of combinations: units, ≥ ≤, equations, physics constants, π, text art; sometimes "big", sometimes "small", sometimes a letter, sometimes the whole name; leisurely at rest and faster the faster the pointer moves | The wordmark flips like a split-flap board through hundreds of combinations (UX-SPEC §1): either half replaced, opposite pairs, look-alike letters, whole equations, constants, and conversions, and text art; 1.8 s at rest, down to about 0.1 s with fast movement; tap to play on phones; still under reduced motion; the name stays "bigsmall" for screen readers |
| Original art in each theme's colors: a sun, a moon, a cherry blossom, a neon skyline, a hard hat, a chalkboard                                                                                                                                              | `ThemeArt`: one SVG per theme drawn for bigsmall, in three art tokens each theme defines; from `sm` up, since phones have no room                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |

Budget: 89.2 KB of 90 KB JavaScript. React's runtime is about 64 KB of it; the decision on Preact or another budget is open (docs/PLAN.md §16).

## Tenth pass: the wordmark tuned, and Preact

Sean's feedback on the first wordmark at play and its art (2026-10-06). He loves all six drawings, and after the checkpoint he kept the resting pace: the point was that nothing gets cut off, smaller type being fine.

| Feedback                                                                             | Change                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Descenders ("g") are sometimes cut off                                               | Nothing clips any more. Each phrase takes the largest type step that fits the name's width, and a flipping letter is never wider than the room its phrase leaves, so every frame fits by construction (docs/LEARNINGS.md)                                                                                        |
| Equations, analogies, and idioms can be smaller so they fit; a size mismatch is fine | Type steps from the name's size down to `sm`, and from `base` down a long phrase takes two lines first ("big fish," over "small pond"), never breaking a grouped number. A browser test fails if any phrase in any theme is too long even then                                                                   |
| Loves analogies like whale:krill and big:small; wants many more, and more equations  | Twelve kinds of phrase with tunable weights: 54 analogies of size, 42 idioms of size and measure (with unit puns: "byte-sized", "ohm sweet ohm"), 50 equations, 30 constants, 65 conversions, 41 plays on the name, beside the swaps, pairs, and text art; over a thousand in all. Figures from SI, CODATA, NIST |
| Changes and flicker too fast while just hovering                                     | Withdrawn after the checkpoint: the resting pace stays. Every timing is on the lab's sliders                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| A debug page with axes and values to tune                                            | `/dev/wordmark-lab`: sliders for every timing, the pointer response, each kind's weight, the art's chances, and the type steps; play without hovering, hold, show any phrase, simulate pointer speed; a sheet of every phrase as it fits in the page theme; "Copy settings" prints the values as code            |
| Only hovering the name and its art should play it, not the menus                     | The hover zone is the heading                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| The art can swap in and out too                                                      | Now and then the art flips to a symbol or short expression in the theme's accent (π, ℏ, c²), landing after the name's letters, and flips back; it always comes home with the name                                                                                                                                |

**Decision: Preact.** A spike on its own branch ran the island on Preact through `preact/compat`: 89.2 KB of JavaScript became 33.9 KB, the converter was ready sooner on a slowed CPU (317 ms against 444), and the full suite passed after five small source changes. Sean chose it over raising the budget; the budget stays at 90 KB, so the room goes to the product (ADR 0007). With the richer wordmark the page ships the amount in docs/PLAN.md §9.

## Twelfth pass: the pre-launch tidy-up and launch prep

Sean's decisions (2026-10-07): `bigsmall.work` is the main domain and `bigsmall.diy` redirects to it, hosting is Vercel (account `reelatlas`, ADR 0008), the analytics tool is chosen later with him, the toolbar stays flush left, and the rest of the roadmap waits for its own session. Before launch he asked for a hygiene pass: logic, ideas, and learnings up to date and consistent; repeatable structures instead of one-off fixes; stale code gone; and anything that needs re-evaluating (patterns, tokens, components, styles, formats, fonts, variants, docs) settled now, so the system is one to build from.

Two read-only audits, the docs against the code and the code for dead parts and one-offs, gave the list. What came of it:

| Area          | Outcome                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| ------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Tests         | One browser spec per feature (smoke, header, wordmark, converter, calculator) around shared helpers in `e2e/support.ts`                                                                                                                |
| Files         | The wordmark's DOM script sits in `lib/` beside its data; the dev pages' folders are named after them (`_gallery`, `_wordmark-lab`); the leftover worktree is gone                                                                     |
| Class recipes | One home, `lib/recipes.ts`, for every class list two files share, and one way to join them (`cx()`, `class:list`); the built HTML is unchanged                                                                                         |
| Page weight   | Astro shipped the components' notes in every page (3.6 KB of the converter page's HTML); they moved into frontmatter, and a test keeps them out                                                                                        |
| Named numbers | Tolerances, margins, hash primes, swap counts, the frame time, and the timing floors have names and a line each                                                                                                                        |
| Duplicates    | One number environment, one table of "no answer" messages, one copy check (`CopiedIcon`) and copy function, one mapping from a search result to a list row, one page header (`PageHeader`), and a layout for the dev pages (`DevPage`) |
| Dead code     | Unused props, markers, and exports removed; core's category groups stay as the catalog's planned taxonomy                                                                                                                              |
| CI            | The browser suite writes the HTML report that the upload on failure expected                                                                                                                                                           |
| Docs          | Every doc read against the code: stale statements fixed, durable learnings promoted into the docs that own them                                                                                                                        |
| Dependencies  | Current under ADR 0005: Preact 11 and TypeScript 7 still wait on their integrations                                                                                                                                                    |

Requests during the pass:

| Request                                                                                                                                                                              | Change                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| "Copy URL" says what the header's copy button does more plainly than "Copy link"                                                                                                     | Its label, tooltip, and confirmation say Copy URL and "URL copied"                                                                                                                                              |
| Moving the mouse over an open list scrolled it; touch, the wheel, and the scrollbar should                                                                                           | Pointing at a row highlights it where it is, and only the arrow keys bring a row into view; the wheel, trackpad, scrollbar, and touch scroll as anywhere. A browser test checks both edges                      |
| The preview card: the name, then a lowercase line under it (first "tap tap tap unit conversion", then "efficient unit converter"; may change later), and a page description to match | The card reads "bigsmall" over "efficient unit converter" (`SITE.tagline`), and the description search results and link previews show is "An efficient unit converter." (`SITE.description`, `src/lib/site.ts`) |
| The not-found sentence: "Address may be mistyped or the page may have moved.", one sentence without commas                                                                           | Done                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| A little more room between the hint and the toolbar row under it                                                                                                                     | 4 px became 8 px; 12 px was tried and set aside, 8 px for now (Sean may revisit)                                                                                                                                |

**Launch prep (ADR 0008).** Decided with Sean: no feedback link for now (friends reply directly), and the `/dev` pages stay on the live site, hidden from search. Built: `site` set to `https://bigsmall.work`; the converter page titled "bigsmall · unit converter"; canonical links and link previews; a sitemap without the `/dev` pages, and `robots.txt`; a not-found page; a mark (a big circle and a small one on one baseline, a proposal Sean may change), with the favicon, app icon, and preview image drawn from the Daylight tokens by a script; the "Component gallery" link off the converter page; `vercel.json` with the build settings and headers; and the runbook, docs/DEPLOYMENT.md, with Sean's one-time Vercel and Porkbun steps. Live on 2026-10-07: `https://bigsmall.work` serves the MVP, tagged `v0.1.0`, and `www.bigsmall.work`, `bigsmall.diy`, and `www.bigsmall.diy` redirect to it with a 308, keeping the path and the conversion in the query.

## Thirteenth pass: after launch

Sean tried the live site (2026-10-07) and raised two things.

| Observation                                                                                                                                                     | Outcome                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| In Chrome, Tab and Shift+Tab eventually leave the page for the browser's own controls (the tabs, Ask Gemini, the address bar); should focus stay in the app?    | No change. That is the browser's own Tab cycle, and every site works this way. Holding focus inside the page would be a keyboard trap (WCAG 2.1.2): someone on a keyboard could never reach the address bar or another tab. Only a modal dialog holds focus, and only while it is open. Inside the converter, Tab already goes from box to box (UX-SPEC §8) |
| With a number and a unit in a box, Enter should move on to the other box, so the next unit can be chosen at once                                                | Enter moves the caret on when a unit in the other box is all the conversion still needs, and that box's list opens inside the category. With both units chosen it stays put, so units can still be compared in one box. A click or tap on a row does not move it; whether it should is a question for the usability test (UX-SPEC §15)                      |
| Confirmed the hand-off is what he expected. Once every unit is chosen, Enter does nothing; it should open the list again, so Enter closes it and Enter opens it | On a closed list with nothing to commit or work out, Enter opens the list, resting on the box's unit, as focus does. A typed unit or a sum in a closed box is still committed or worked out first                                                                                                                                                           |
| After Enter turns a sum into its value (`6*3` → `18`) in a box with no unit, the list should open so a unit can be chosen                                       | The list stays open, or opens, after the sum is worked out, since a unit comes next. A plain number with no unit still closes the list on Enter, so Enter always does something visible                                                                                                                                                                     |

| Let users choose the fraction sizes they care about (1/2, 1/3, 1/4, 1/8, 1/16, 1/32, 1/64), each showing all its values                                     | Several sizes at once, each writing its whole grid; a finer size holds the coarser it contains, which show chosen and locked; thirds mix with the rest                                                                     |
| For 32nds and 64ths, offer "most common" or all                                                                                                             | Proposed instead: all. The table writes one value per row, so a common set would hide nothing and only round some values further than asked; the hardware sizes behind it are a lookup for a later feature                 |
| Opening Hidden indents the shown values                                                                                                                     | The Hidden list has columns of its own, so the table never moves                                                                                                                                                           |
| 3/64 or 4/64 next to 1/16: reduced, or ever unreduced?                                                                                                      | Reduced, as drill bits, tapes, and charts mark them; unreduced only helps counting a rule's ticks, a later option if asked                                                                                                 |
| Show decimals and fractions side by side?                                                                                                                   | Recommended as a third Format choice, Both; asked, not built                                                                                                                                                               |

## Forty-third pass: the number pad, names by default, the foot's spacing, an inline clear (saved before a compaction, built the same day)

Sean's notes on the live forty-second pass (2026-10-15), saved before a compaction with the plan for each, then built the same day after the compaction, with Sean's answers below the table (everything before it was live at `fba392a`).

| Observation                                                                                         | Plan                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| On a phone, open the number keyboard rather than letters (Sean: yes to the recommendation, for now) | `inputmode="decimal"` on the box only while a unit is in it (the chip) and the category is not Temperature: then the box needs a number alone, and units are picked from the table or the menu, or the chip's × brings the full keyboard back. With no unit, or in Temperature (negatives such as −40), the full keyboard, so "2 cups" and "-40" still type. An iPhone's decimal pad has no letters, no minus, no "+", and no key back to letters, so it never shows where those are needed. The mode takes effect on the next focus. ValueField; UX-SPEC §1 and ADR 0015 (the touch keyboard) amended; a touch test in `touch.spec.ts` asserting the attribute in each case |
| Remember Display as well as Units                                                                   | Confirmed: as built (`settings-store.ts`), no change                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| The Hidden row at the table's foot: its hover fill overlaps the divider line; best practice?        | The spacing trial (`fba392a`) set the foot's padding to balance the text about the line (`mt-0.5 … pt-0.5`), which left the 44 px buttons' hover fill 2 px under it. Rule to add to "Sections and dividers, by what shows": a control that shows a fill (hovered, switched on: Hidden, Edit and Done, the panels' resets) keeps at least 4 px between its fill and a divider, the fill being what shows. Set the table's foot `pt-1` (and check Done switched on), Units' and Display's feet likewise, re-measure with the audit script (scratchpad `spacing-audit.mjs`), and extend the spacing test to the fills                                                           |
| Make Symbol and name the fresh visit's default rather than Symbol only                              | `FRESH_DISPLAY.names = 'both'`. Remembered settings keep what a visitor chose. Update UX-SPEC §1 ("A fresh visit's settings"), ADR 0017 (amended), the e2e tests that assume symbols alone on a fresh visit (settings.spec's first test, `showEverything`, and any asserting a hidden name), and the gallery samples if they rely on the default                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| Should Symbol only and Symbol and name live in Units instead of Display?                            | Recommend no (to bring to Sean): Units decides which units show, Display how each row is written; names are how a row reads, so they stay in Display, beside the format. Moving them would mix the two panels' jobs                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| An × in the box to clear the number; best practice, or too many ×s with the eraser and the chip?    | Recommend not now (to bring to Sean): "no inline clear" was decided 2026-10-08 (UX-SPEC §14); the chip's × already sits at the field's right end, and a second × beside it would be confused with it (one clears the number, one the unit); clear all is in the toolbar, Escape steps back one thing at a time, and on a phone selecting the text and deleting works. If a clear is still wanted on phones, the better form is the eraser clearing the number first and everything on a second press, or the × appearing only while there is no unit chip. Record Sean's answer in §14                                                                                       |
| Keep "Your region" (do not shorten to "Region")                                                     | Keep: the side column stays 320 px (`296e39d`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |

| Observation                                                                                                                                                                                                                  | What changed                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| The inline clear, rethought: the chip's × sits inside the chip, so a × in the input reads as the input's; clearing just the number will be used often, and only Escape does it; the eraser outside the input stays clear all | Agreed, and built as a trial in its own commit: a × in a circle (`ClearIcon`) right after the text, before the chip, while the box holds text. It takes the text out and keeps the unit, one undoable step ("Clear number", or "Clear text" with a unit typed after the number); a press never takes focus from the box, so a phone's keyboard that is up stays up, and a tap never brings it up (ADR 0015). The earlier worry, two ×s side by side, is met by the placement (the chip's name between them) and the glyph. UX-SPEC §1, §8, §14                                                                                                                                                                                 |

## Tidy pass, full, 2026-10-15 (before a fresh session and v1)

Scope: the whole system, `packages/core`, `apps/web` (source, e2e, unit tests), every doc, `CLAUDE.md`, and the skills, audited in five parts by read-only reviewers in parallel and each finding checked by reading before acting. Gates and the browser suite green before and after. Sean's notes during the pass were built first, each its own commit: the page's ends as stops for ↑ and ↓, the shortcuts map opening on a click, its icon's focus ring and switched-on fill, and ← and → leaving an open menu.

- **Fixed.** Code: shared recipes for the side panels' card and title row and for a page's main column, the table's section label from `CURSOR_ROW`, the single-use shell styles beside their uses, dead code out (`groupState`, an unused prop, a dead `className`), stale and ragged comments current. Tests: one locale on any machine, a hydration test that can fail on a build, helpers shared, the map's line found by a data mark, motion lengths from the theme, geometry and fill gaps in every theme. Docs: the spec, design system, architecture, testing, plan, catalog, ADR index, ADR 0017's amendments gathered, research docs settled; LEARNINGS indexed by topic with promotions; this log indexed; `CLAUDE.md` lean, with "Pick up here". Skills: tidy taught this session's lessons; `ship` and `save` added. Editor: Tailwind's IntelliSense reads `cx()` and the recipe constants; Markdown links are checked.
- **Open with Sean** (recommendation first):
  1. "Standard" names both the preset (everything but Specialty) and the heading of a category with no regional system (Time, Data, Angle): rename the heading ("Everyday"), or drop it where it is a category's only everyday section.
  2. The matches' revealed Hidden group lacks the line every other group after the first has: add it.
  3. Long names are cut with an ellipsis in Edit's and Hidden's lines and among hidden matches, while ordinary matches wrap: let them wrap.
  4. Round to's labels (0.1, 0.01, 0.001) are not in the numeric face, while the fraction sizes are: set them in it (it shows in the monospace themes).
  5. The chip's hover tint (`hover:bg-surface/40`) is a one-off alpha the token guard does not catch: name it as an allowed modifier in DESIGN-SYSTEM, or make it a token.
  6. The header uses two viewport breakpoints (the shortcuts map's `sm:block`, the theme label's `max-sm:`) where the system prefers a container query: move them to the header's container.
  7. The chip's accessible name reads "Change unit: pints" though a click only puts the caret in the box; Edit/Done takes the switched-on fill without `aria-pressed` (its label changing says the state): rename the chip's name ("pints, in the box"), and record Edit/Done as the exception.
  8. Three rules keep coming back and could go into `CLAUDE.md`'s non-negotiables: verify motion and CSS on the built site; measure, never count or tune to one machine; commit only on `pnpm check`'s exit status (already in the ship skill).
  9. ADR 0017 has grown by many amendments in one day (side panels, Custom, the order, remembered settings, copy): write ADR 0018 for the settings as they stand, or keep amending.
  10. Prettier could sort classes inside `cx()` too (`tailwindFunctions`): it reformats many files, so one commit of its own.
  11. Tests that would run every theme (Both's fraction under the decimal at 360 px, the long-number sweeps) make the suite slower; and the glide's length is read after a wait a slow CI could outrun: worth it, or accept the risk.
  12. Trials still awaiting a verdict: the Units icon (`334b2eb`), the box's × (`0178c30`), the spacing rule (`fba392a`), `--edge`, the mark and preview card.
- **Logged for later.** `docs/PLAN.md` §2, "Hygiene backlog": one seam recipe for the Move menu and the segmented control; the calculator built on `SidePanel`; the header menus' placement in their parent; the header's width check (351 or 353 px); one shared `MINUS`; core's unused public API; two gallery samples and seven tests still missing; UX-SPEC §14's built items.
- **Extensions.** The six the repo recommends are right for this stack. Worth adding: Code Spell Checker (`streetsidesoftware.code-spell-checker`), for a docs-heavy repo with strict copy rules, once a `cspell.json` lists the unit symbols and theme names. Not worth it: an axe linter (Playwright's axe and jsx-a11y already gate), markdownlint (Prettier formats the docs and its line rule fights the tables).

| The manual accessibility passes                                                                                                                 | Explained: once, just before launch, VoiceOver on Sean's iPhone and NVDA from a checklist, the keyboard and zoom passes scripted                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| The accuracy audit                                                                                                                              | After the rest of this list                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| Space on Hidden, Edit, and Show them: should it type into the box, Enter alone pressing?                                                        | Recommended the standard, and Sean agreed: Space and Enter press a focused button and Space ticks a checkbox, as keyboard and screen-reader users expect everywhere. The case it would help is a Space pressed first on a foot button; a letter or digit typed there already lands in the box, and the focus ring shows what Space presses. No change |

**Analytics, the options** (researched 2026-10-15 from each vendor's own pages; prices change, so confirm before choosing). The converter page ships 62 KB of its 90 KB JavaScript budget, so a script's size counts.

- **Umami Cloud, recommended.** One 2.3 KB script: traffic, product events with properties (category, the pair, the calculator, Copy URL, theme, settings), and real-user speed (Core Web Vitals, opt-in). Cookieless, so no banner; export on every plan; served from `bigsmall.work` through one Vercel rewrite, so ad blockers drop less. Hobby is free for one site up to 100k events a month (each stored property counts as an event); Pro is $20 a month for up to 20 sites, 1M events, two years of history, and the API. MIT-licensed, so self-hosting stays an exit. To confirm on its trial: that speed tracking is on the plan, and how it counts.
- **Vercel Web Analytics and Speed Insights, the runner-up.** Already on the account, cookieless, served from the site's own domain by default, billed from the Pro plan's credit (about $0.03 per 1,000 events). Two properties per event on Pro (eight with a $10 add-on); simple event reports; real-user speed figures need Speed Insights Plus, $10 per project plus usage. The cheapest layer for traffic and events if every site stays on Vercel.
- **Not a fit.** Plausible: polished, no free plan, event properties only on its Business plan, no speed. Fathom and GoatCounter: no event properties, and neither serves from the site's own domain. Simple Analytics and Pirsch: no speed. Cloudflare Web Analytics: no custom events. PostHog: a 105 KB script (50 KB slim) and, without cookies, no countries. Google Analytics 4: cookies and a consent banner. Supabase is a database, not analytics: it would mean building the collector, bot filtering, visitor counting, and dashboards, its only sensible role being the database behind a self-hosted Umami.
- **Beside any of them.** Google Search Console for search (free, verified by a DNS record); errors through Sentry's free plan, loaded only when needed; uptime through UptimeRobot's free plan.
- **For several sites.** Umami Cloud Pro ($20, 20 sites, any host), or Vercel Web Analytics while every site is on Vercel.

## Fifty-first pass: the sketchbook's changes, in the spirit of the name at play

Sean on the sketchbook lab (2026-10-15), following the fiftieth pass. Live at `8046395` before it.

| Observation                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          | Plan, and what changed                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Pretty cool, but he had in mind something more straightforward: the header's transition, glitching in and out; something cool that feels nice. Keep every sketch in the rotation; then: keep the options for modifying the transition and the effects, and add different transitions | Kept every option and effect, and added four that echo the name at play, first in the Effect list: **Glitch**, the new default (bands of the sketch jump sideways and its edges split into the theme's accent and art colors, now and then a band of another sketch flicking by, out of the old sketch and into the new, settling as the name's letters land); **Scramble** (the next sketch lands block by block in a four-by-four grid, the next few blocks flipping through other sketches, as each letter flips through symbols); **Flap** (a split-flap board's card: the top half falls toward the viewer and the new bottom half follows, each darkening as it turns); **Scan** (drawn in row by row, a bright row leading). Two new controls: **Frame**, how often the stepped ones show a new frame, as the name's Flip does, at the name's 34 ms to start; and **Glitch strength**. Built to stay under WCAG 2.3.1's flash threshold whatever the lab sets: a change swaps a tile's whole picture once, the frame-to-frame flicker is at most a quarter of a tile (`FLICKER_SHARE`, tested), and a tile playing by itself changes at most three times a second (`SKETCH_MIN_GAP_MS`). Every sketch stays in the rotation, as before |

## Fifty-sixth pass: after the compaction, a full tidy, the accuracy audit, and categories to add (saved before a compaction; built in the fifty-seventh and the tidy after it)

## Tidy pass, full, 2026-10-15 (after the sketchbook)

Scope: the whole system, `packages/core` (source and tests), `apps/web` (source, browser tests, unit tests), every doc, `CLAUDE.md`, the README, and the skills, audited in five parts by read-only reviewers in parallel (tokens, recipes, and components; behavior against the spec; dead code and comments; tests; docs and skills), the sketchbook and what came with it first, and each finding checked by reading before acting. Gates and the browser suite green before and after (206 browser tests before, 212 after). Sean's notes during the pass were built first, each its own commit: ADR 0020, the sketchbook's longest wait (v3), and the formulas page logged. The accuracy audit ran beside it (docs/research/accuracy-audit-1.md).

- **Fixed.** Docs (`e75a3e2`): UX-SPEC's stale lines (No limit, the copy, the flicker rate's scope, the shortcuts' letters, the chip's spoken names), ADR 0014's amendments and 0012's outcome, the sketchbook's exceptions to the token guard said in one place, the recipes and the gallery in DESIGN-SYSTEM, Shuffle and the labs' storage in ARCHITECTURE, TESTING's spec rows and the preview procedure in one home, PLAN's and the catalog's drift (four everyday units listed as specialist), the learnings' notes, the README's two boxes and modes, the tidy skill's scope. Code (`d8b9d51`, `1ab3c44`): the player's dead `next()`, `Family.round`, an unread data mark, and defaults that never applied, out; `shuffle()` named `flip()`; the safety gaps as pure functions (`restMs`, `idleWaitMs`); shared recipes (`PAGE_COLUMN`, `GROUP_TITLE`, `ROW_RULE`, `unitName()`, `unitSymbol()` holding its `whitespace-nowrap`); the gallery's links; stale and ragged comments. Tests (`4e11d7c`): three that could not fail now can; lengths asserted against the theme (`themeTime`); particles and the pace pinned; new tests for Flip all at once under reduced motion, what a press and Flip say, the lab's earlier versions, and the About page's Tab stops.
- **Open with Sean** (recommendation first; settled in the fifty-eighth pass):
  1. **The therm** (docs/research/accuracy-audit-1.md): 100,000 International Table BTU (105,505,585.262 J), as the EIA, gas bills, and the UK define it, so 1 therm reads as 100,000 BTU; today it is the US legal therm, 99,976 BTU. 0.024 % more. Alternative: keep the legal therm and say so in its note.
  2. **Ties below zero**: away from zero in places and fractions too (−2.5 to −3, −0.125 to −0.13, −0.5 to −1), as above zero and as the table's own digits round; today they round up (−2.5 to −2, −0.5 to "> −1").
  3. **A pause for the About page's tiles** (WCAG 2.2.2, Level A: something that moves by itself for more than five seconds needs a way to stop it): a quiet Pause beside Flip that stops them changing by themselves. Alternative: they change by themselves only for a while after the page opens or a press.
  4. **The flicker limit between frames**: Glitch's bands and Scramble's blocks change up to half a tile from one frame to the next, where the docs promise a quarter (still under WCAG 2.3.1's area at the shipped size). Make them meet it, a little calmer, with a test across frames. Alternative: say per frame and cap the lab's tile size.
  5. **The link home 44 px tall** (it is 36 off the converter's page): `py-1 -my-1` on it, nothing moving, its focus ring 8 px taller. Alternative: record it as an exception (well over WCAG 2.5.8's 24 px).
  6. **The mark first on the About page**: R6 in the middle of the first three tiles, so it shows even under reduced motion, where the page does not flip as it opens. Today they open on Cat and kitten, Nesting dolls, and Balloons.
  7. **The shortcuts icon only on the converter's page**, where its shortcuts work; today About, the not-found page, and the labs show it too.
  8. **One weight for counts beside labels**, the regular one, in Hidden, Units' reset, and the matches' groups (the matches' are medium today).
  9. **Flip's marks**: their ring takes the button's fill at the press; on the hovered fill it shows as a crescent.
  10. **The spring curve stays a lab choice** (`SPRING_EASING`), at a few bytes. Alternative: drop it.
  11. **The commit gate said once**, in CLAUDE.md's Working habits, the Ship recipe, Commits, and the skills linking there; today it is written five times.
  12. **The dev pages' footer** sits up to 64 px in from their wider column: leave it, the pages being ours alone.
- **Logged for later.** `docs/PLAN.md` §2, "Hygiene backlog": the labs' shared controls and their shipped marks, the wordmark lab's settings through an effect, one name for the shipped settings, the lab's preview of reduced motion for Flip, long names in every theme, a sweep of widths, the lab's migration untested, a budget for the About page, the matches' line against the table's foot, and one home each for the sketchbook's behavior and the recipes.

Scrolling fallback measured, never counted: on open and on resize, the list's `max-height` is the room from its top to the window's bottom, less 16 px, `overflow-y: auto`; the chosen category scrolled into view inside the list (`scrollTop`), the page never. Counts while typing stay at each choice's right. UX-SPEC §1 (the category menu), DESIGN-SYSTEM "Fluid shells", ARCHITECTURE. The box: "2 cups flour" reads the amount, the unit, and the ingredient, two pills, each removable (without the ingredient, Volume; without the unit, the ingredient waits for an amount); the ingredient pill opens a list of ingredients. The table: Weight, then Volume, by the visitor's system within each; the cup said under it (the US cup for a US visitor, the metric 250 mL elsewhere). The ingredient in the URL (`i=`), so a link keeps it. Thirty staples to start (the lab's list). The categories lab keeps the mocks until it ships. Other categories with a second value, Cooking's shape, to the roadmap: oven temperatures with gas marks and fan ovens (with Cooking), download time (a file's size over a connection's speed), price per unit (per pound against per kilogram), and print size at a resolution (pixels against inches at a dpi) |

No forced "+": the change keeps the number's own sign, so a typed −25 °F reads −13.89 °C. Shown by default, not under Specialty; Units has no switch for it yet (the filter exploration below). **Built the same day** (Sean, after the compaction: "proceed with all your recommendations", the sticky bar for Hide all his choice over a fixed height), each on the gate, pushed, and passing CI:

- **The menu in two columns** (`aaaf332`): open, twice as wide as the menu, down the first column and on into the second, held to the window (its bottom 8 px above the window's, never under four rows) and scrolling inside, the chosen category scrolled into the list's view, never the page. The lab's menu section retired, a screenshot kept (`docs/research/labs/categories-lab/`). A sketchbook test that waited on a comma, which one sketch's name holds, fixed on the way (`f12ed97`).
- **Every formula reworked** (`652b920`): Sean's lines, the counts in the ink, Show all a disclosure with the categories' chevron, in a bar that stays at the window's top while the open list scrolls past; "The sketchbook" heading (a shared `SECTION_HEADING`); the centered column; "7 units in newton meters".
- **Standard and specialty** (`5c924ef`): in the docs that describe today and, on Sean's mid-turn question ("should the code say standard too?"), in the code as well: `tier: 'standard' | 'specialty'`, the Specialty section's key `specialty`, the category family `common`. Saved settings moved to version 4, reading the old key, so no one's switches or order are lost; a learning ("One idea, one name").
- **Pace** (`4d9be07`, audit `542083f`): min/km and min/mi in a Pace section after the speeds. Departures from the plan, for the better: the box reads a clock's time as each part sixtieths of the one before, whatever the unit (`1:30 h` is an hour and a half), so no special case for paces; and a pace past an hour stays in minutes (`65:30`), so what the table writes reads back the same. A pace under a second reads `< 0:01`. The link keeps `5:30` as typed. Both factors checked blind and agreeing; the mile's now cites NIST SP 811 Appendix B.9 directly.
- **A temperature change** (`fd10350`): after the readings, "As a change: 5 °C warmer or cooler" (a negative amount says its direction itself), every scale but the box's own, by the scales' degree sizes alone (core's `convertChange`), so no units of difference were needed beside the scales. Left out of Edit; a change row takes its scale into the box.
- **Cooking** (`58d8365`, ADR 0021): as planned, with the box's two pills wrapping as one under the number where a phone lacks the room; 34 staples checked blind (`docs/research/cooking-1.md`); Cooking after Volume in the menu and the tiles. Left for later: the metric cup, weights to the gram, instant yeast. The categories lab now holds only Cooking's mocks, and retires with Sean's go.

The pill in the box stays the readout, as the unit chip does beside the category menu; switching keeps the amount and the unit. (b) The list Cooking shows with no ingredient, grouped the same way.
